Lastly, there exists an absolute constant
C_{2}>0
such that

\displaystyle\Pi_{n,p}(\|v\|_{0},\|\theta\|_{0},t_{\|v\|_{0},\|\theta\|_{0},n,\delta})\leq C_{2}\sqrt{t_{\|v\|_{0},\|\theta\|_{0},n,\delta}}\sqrt{1+\pi_{n,1}^{2}(t_{\|v\|_{0},\|\theta\|_{0},n,\delta})}.

This completes the proof of case (i).

Proof of Case (ii).
Observe that
s\mapsto s\log(ep/s)
and
s\mapsto\varphi_{\max}(s)
are monotone increasing on
[1,p]
. Thus, the bound of case (i) for
g_{v,\theta,\tau}\in\mathcal{G}
with
\|v\|_{0}=\|\theta\|_{0}=m
holds also for all
g_{v^{\prime},\theta^{\prime},\tau^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{G}
with
\|v^{\prime}\|_{0},\|\theta^{\prime}\|_{0}\leq m
. To conclude, adjust some constants.
∎

Proof of Lemma
17
.

Fix
k\leq n\wedge p
, fix
I\subseteq\{1,\ldots,n\}
with
\mathrm{card}(I)=k
, and fix the support set of
u\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
with
\|u\|_{0}=\ell
. By Lemma
14
with
\vartheta=0
, there exists an absolute constant
C>0
such that with probability at least
1-2e^{-t}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\|u\|_{2}\leq 1}\left|\frac{1}{k}\sum_{i\in I}(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}-\mathbb{E}[(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}]\right|\leq C\varphi_{\max}(\ell)\left(\sqrt{\frac{\ell+t}{k}}+\frac{\ell+t}{k}\right).

Note that
\mathrm{card}\big(\{I\subseteq\{1,\ldots,n\}:\mathrm{card}(I)\leq k\}\big)=\sum_{i=1}^{k}{n\choose i}\leq\left(\frac{en}{k}\right)^{k}
and
\mathrm{card}\big(\{u\in\{0,1\}^{p}:\|u\|_{0}\leq k\}\big)=\sum_{i=1}^{k}{p\choose i}\leq\left(\frac{ep}{k}\right)^{k}
. Therefore, setting
t_{k,\ell,n}=k\log(ep/k)+k\log(en/k)+\log(2n/\delta)
and applying the union bound gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\exists k\leq n:\frac{\sup_{\mathrm{card}(I)\leq k}\sup_{\ell\leq k}\sup_{\|u\|_{2}\leq 1,\|u\|_{0}=\ell}\left|\frac{1}{k}\sum_{i\in I}(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}-\mathbb{E}[(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}]\right|}{\varphi_{\max}(\ell)\left(\sqrt{\frac{\ell+t_{k,\ell,n}}{k}}+\frac{\ell+t_{k,\ell,n}}{k}\right)}\geq C\right\}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\leq 2\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\frac{ep}{k}\right)^{k}\left(\frac{en}{k}\right)^{k}e^{-t_{k,\ell,n}}\leq\delta.

Upper bound
\ell
by
k
, simplify the expression, and conclude.
∎

I.4
Proofs of Section
G.3

Proof of Lemma
18
.

Proof of statement (i).
To simplify the discussion we introduce the following matrices and vectors

\displaystyle\widehat{\Psi}(\tau)

\displaystyle=2\>\mathrm{diag}\left(\hat{f}_{1}^{-2}(\tau),\>\ldots,\>\hat{f}_{n}^{-2}(\tau)\right)\>\>\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n},

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=n^{-1/2}\left[-\mathbf{X},\>\mathbf{X}\right]^{\prime}\>\>\in\mathbb{R}^{2p\times n},

\displaystyle b

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{\gamma}{n}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-z^{\prime},\>\frac{\gamma}{n}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+z^{\prime}\right]^{\prime}\>\>\in\mathbb{R}^{2p},

and rewrite the convex optimization problem (
27
) in standard matrix form as

\displaystyle\min_{w\in\mathbb{R}^{n}}\>\>\>

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}w^{\prime}\widehat{\Psi}(\tau)w

\displaystyle\mathrm{s.t.\>\>\>}

\displaystyle Aw\preceq b.

Recall that the dual of above optimization problem is given by

\displaystyle\max_{\lambda\in\mathbb{R}^{2p}}\>\>\>

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\lambda^{\prime}A\widehat{\Psi}^{-1}(\tau)A^{\prime}\lambda-\lambda^{\prime}b

\displaystyle\mathrm{s.t.\>\>\>}

\displaystyle\lambda\succeq 0.

By assumption the primal problem is feasible. Thus, strong duality holds and the solution to the primal,
\widehat{w}(\tau;z)\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
, and the solution to the dual,
\hat{\lambda}(\tau)\in\mathbb{R}^{2p}
, satisfy

\displaystyle\widehat{w}(\tau;z)=-\widehat{\Psi}^{-1}(\tau)A^{\prime}\hat{\lambda}(\tau).

(78)

Since
\hat{\lambda}(\tau)\in\mathbb{R}^{2p}
is just the vector of optimal Lagrange multipliers associated with the original problem formulation in (
27
), it also satisfies the complementary slackness conditions

\displaystyle\hat{\lambda}_{k}(\tau)\geq 0\hskip 20.0pt\mathrm{and}\hskip 20.0pt\big[A\widehat{w}(\tau)\big]_{k}<b_{k}\implies\hat{\lambda}_{k}(\tau)=0,\hskip 20.0ptk=1,\ldots,2p.

Write
\hat{\lambda}(\tau)=\left(\overline{\mu},\>\underline{\mu}\right)
, where
\overline{\mu},\underline{\mu}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}_{+}
are the optimal Lagrange multipliers associate with the box constraints of COP (
27
). The complementary slackness conditions imply that

\displaystyle\overline{\mu}_{k}\underline{\mu}_{k}=0,\hskip 9.24994pt{}k=1,\ldots,p.

Set
\hat{v}(\tau;z):=\left(-\overline{\mu}+\underline{\mu}\right)\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
. Above identity implies that

\displaystyle|\hat{v}_{k}(\tau;z)|=\overline{\mu}_{k}+\underline{\mu}_{k},\hskip 9.24994pt{}k=1,\ldots,p,

and hence

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\hat{\lambda}^{\prime}(\tau)A\widehat{\Psi}^{-1}(\tau)A^{\prime}\hat{\lambda}(\tau)-\hat{\lambda}^{\prime}(\tau)b=-\frac{1}{2n}\hat{v}^{\prime}(\tau;z)\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\widehat{\Psi}^{-1}(\tau)\mathbf{X}\hat{v}(\tau;z)-z^{\prime}\hat{v}(\tau;z)-\frac{\gamma}{n}\|\hat{v}(\tau;z)\|_{1}.

(79)

Since every
v\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
can be written as
v=\left(-\overline{\mu}+\underline{\mu}\right)
for some
\overline{\mu},\underline{\mu}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}_{+}
, identity (
79
) implies that
\hat{v}(\tau;z)
is the solution to the unconstrained COP

\displaystyle\min_{v\in\mathbb{R}^{p}}\frac{1}{2n}v^{\prime}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\widehat{\Psi}^{-1}(\tau)\mathbf{X}v+z^{\prime}v+\frac{\gamma}{n}\|v\|_{1}.

Lastly, note that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2n}v^{\prime}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\widehat{\Psi}^{-1}(\tau)\mathbf{X}v=\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(X_{i}^{\prime}v\big)^{2}.

This concludes the proof of the first statement.

Proof of statement (ii).
Note that the primal problem
27
is convex. To establish the claim it is therefore sufficient to verify that Slater’s condition holds with probability at least
1-\delta
. To this end, define

\displaystyle w^{*}(z)=n^{-1/2}\mathbf{X}\mathbb{E}[XX^{\prime}]^{-1}z.

Note that
n^{-1/2}\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{X}^{\prime}w^{*}(z)]=z
. Hence, by Assumption
1
the
z_{1}-n^{-1/2}e_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}w^{*}(z),\ldots,z_{p}-n^{-1/2}e_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}w^{*}(z)
are centered (non-identical and dependent) sub-exponential random variables. (Here,
e_{k}
denotes the
k
th standard unit vector in
\mathbb{R}^{p}
.) Moreover,
z_{k}-n^{-1/2}e_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}w^{*}(z)=-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(X_{ik}X_{i}^{\prime}\mathbb{E}[XX^{\prime}]^{-1}z-z_{k})
for all
1\leq k\leq p
. By Lemma
36
and Assumption
1
, for all
1\leq i\leq n

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq k\leq p}\|X_{ik}X_{i}^{\prime}\mathbb{E}[XX^{\prime}]^{-1}z-z_{k}\|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\lesssim\max_{1\leq k\leq p}\left\|\ X_{ik}X_{i}^{\prime}\mathbb{E}[XX^{\prime}]^{-1}z\right\|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\lesssim\max_{1\leq k\leq p}\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\|X_{ik}\|_{\psi_{2}}\|X_{i}^{\prime}\mathbb{E}[XX^{\prime}]^{-1}z\|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\lesssim\frac{\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(1)\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}.

Hence, the union bound followed by Bernstein’s inequality implies that for all
t\geq 0
, there exists an absolute constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\big\|z-n^{-1/2}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}w^{*}(z)\big\|_{\infty}>t\right\}

\displaystyle\leq p\max_{1\leq k\leq p}\mathbb{P}\left\{\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(X_{ik}X_{i}^{\prime}\mathbb{E}[XX^{\prime}]^{-1}z-z_{k})\right|>t\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2p\exp\left(-C\min\left\{\frac{t\kappa_{2}(\infty)}{\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(1)\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\|z\|_{2}},\>\frac{t^{2}\kappa_{2}(\infty)}{\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(1)\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\|z\|_{2}^{2}}\right\}n\right).

Set
t^{*}=\max\{s,s^{2}\}
with
s=\frac{\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(1)\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}\left(\frac{\log 2p+\log(C/\delta)}{n}\right)^{1/2}
and conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta
the constraint set is non-empty whenever
\gamma>t^{*}
, i.e. Slater’s condition holds. Lastly, observe that by eq. (
78
),

\displaystyle\widehat{w}(\tau;z)=-n^{-1/2}\widehat{\Psi}^{-1}(\tau)\mathbf{X}\hat{v}(\tau;z).

This concludes the proof of the second statement.
∎

Proof of Theorem
5
.

Ansatz.

Let
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
and
r>\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa^{2}_{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\geq 0
(to be specified below). Recall that
B^{p}_{q}(0,r):=\{u\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:\|u\|_{q}\leq r\}
and that
C^{p}_{q}(J,\vartheta)=\left\{u\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:\|u_{J^{c}}\|_{q}\leq\vartheta\|u_{J}\|_{q}\right\}
. To simplify notation, we write
\tilde{v}(\tau)
,
\hat{v}(\tau)
,
T_{v}(\tau)
, and
s_{v}
for
\tilde{v}(\tau;z)
,
\hat{v}(\tau;z)
,
T_{v}(\tau;z)
, and
s_{v}(z)
, respectively.
Define

\displaystyle K(\tau):=\left\{v\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:\begin{matrix}v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\in C^{p}_{1}\big(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c}\big)\cup B^{p}_{1}\left(0,\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right),\\
\bar{c}\sum_{k\in T_{v}(\tau)}\big|v_{k}(\tau)-\tilde{v}_{k}(\tau)\big|+\frac{\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\geq\sum_{k\in T_{v}^{c}(\tau)}\big|v_{k}(\tau)\big|\end{matrix}\right\}.

Suppose that, with high probability,

(a)

\hat{v}(\tau)\in K(\tau)
for all
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
; and

(b)

the centered dual objective function is strictly positive when evaluated at points
(v,\tau)\in\mathbb{R}^{p}\times\mathcal{T}
satisfying
v\in K(r,\tau):=K(\tau)\cap B^{p}_{2}(\tilde{v}(\tau),r)
, i.e.

\displaystyle\inf_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\inf_{v\in K(r,\tau)}\left(\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}v)^{2}-\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(X_{i}^{\prime}\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)^{2}\right)+nz^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)+\gamma\big(\|v\|_{1}-\|\tilde{v}(\tau)\|_{1}\big)\right)>0.

Since the dual objective function is convex in
v
and negative at
\hat{v}(\tau)
for all
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
, it then follows that
\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\|\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)\|_{2}\lesssim r
. Thus, to establish the claim of the theorem, we only need to prove that statements (a) and (b) hold with high probability.

Verification of high probability statements.
By assumption, eq. (
39
) and (
40
) hold true. Thus, by Lemma
24
, statement (a) holds for all
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
with probability one. We now establish statement (b). To this end, we define

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{1}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)v_{0}^{\prime}(\tau)XX^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big),\>v\in K(r,\tau),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big(X^{\prime}(\tilde{v}(\tau)-v)\big)^{2},\>v\in K(r,\tau),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{3}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big(\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}XX^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big),\>v\in K(r,\tau),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\}.

We begin with two preliminary observations.

First, note that by Assumptions
8
and
9
, with probability at least
1-\eta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)-f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\right|&=\left|\big(\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-f_{i}(\tau)\big)\big(\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)+f_{i}(\tau)\big)\right|=\big(\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-f_{i}(\tau)\big)^{2}+2\big|\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-f_{i}(\tau)\big|f_{i}(\tau)\\
&\leq\left|\frac{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}{f_{i}(\tau)}-1\right|^{2}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)+2\left|\frac{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}{f_{i}(\tau)}-1\right|f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\lesssim r_{f}f_{i}^{2}(\tau).\end{split}

(80)

Therefore, with probability at least
1-\eta
, for all
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}v)^{2}-\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(X_{i}^{\prime}\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)^{2}+z^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}=\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\tilde{v}^{\prime}(\tau)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)+\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)+z^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)-f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big)\tilde{v}^{\prime}(\tau)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)+\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)-f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big)\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}=\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)v_{0}(\tau)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)+z^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)+\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)-f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big)v_{0}^{\prime}(\tau)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)+\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)-f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big)\big(\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)-f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big)\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\gtrsim\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}-n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}-n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}-n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{3}}-r_{f}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}-r_{f}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}-r_{f}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{3}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}.

Second, observe that

\displaystyle K(r,\tau)=\left\{v\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:\begin{matrix}v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\in C^{p}_{1}\big(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c}\big),\\
\bar{c}\sum_{k\in T_{v}(\tau)}\big|v_{k}(\tau)-\tilde{v}_{k}(\tau)\big|+\frac{\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\geq\sum_{k\in T_{v}^{c}(\tau)}\big|v_{k}(\tau)\big|\end{matrix}\right\}\cap\partial B^{p}_{2}(0,r),

(81)

since
r>\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa^{2}_{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}
and
B^{p}_{1}\left(0,\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\cap\partial B^{p}_{2}(0,r)\neq\emptyset
only if the radius of the
\ell_{1}
-ball is greater or equal to the radius of the
\ell_{2}
-sphere.

Now, with probability at least
1-\eta
,

\displaystyle\inf_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\inf_{v\in K(r,\tau)}\left(\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}v)^{2}-\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(X_{i}^{\prime}\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)^{2}\right)+z^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)+\frac{\gamma}{n}\big(\|v\|_{1}-\|\tilde{v}(\tau)\|_{1}\big)\right)

\displaystyle\gtrsim\inf_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\inf_{v\in K(r,\tau)}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}-\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{v\in K(r,\tau)}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}-n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}-n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}-n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{3}}-r_{f}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}-r_{f}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}-r_{f}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{3}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}-\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{v\in K(r,\tau)}\frac{\gamma}{n}\big(\|v\|_{1}-\|\tilde{v}(\tau)\|_{1}\big)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{II}-\mathbf{III}-\mathbf{IV}-\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{VI}-\mathbf{VII}-\mathbf{VIII}-\mathbf{IX}.

(82)

In the following, we bound the expressions on the far right hand side in eq. (
I.4
).

Bound on
\mathbf{I}
.
Thus, by eq. (
81
),

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}\gtrsim\inf_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\inf_{u\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c})\cap\partial B^{p}_{2}(0,1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}\right]r^{2}\geq\kappa_{2}(2\bar{c})r^{2}.

(83)

Bound on
\mathbf{II}
.
By Lemma
23
, eq. (
81
), and two applications of Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{v\in K(r,\tau)}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big(\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{v\in K(r,\tau)}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\Big(\big(\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}X_{i}\Big)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\Big(X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big)\Big)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}r_{a}r\times\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{u\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\cap\partial B^{P}_{2}(0,1)}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sup_{u\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c})\cap\partial B^{P}_{2}(0,1)}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}.

By Lemmas
11
,
12
, and
14
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{u\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\cap\partial B^{P}_{2}(0,1)}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\leq 6\bar{f}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v}),

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{u\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c})\cap\partial B^{P}_{2}(0,1)}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\leq 2(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v}),

and, therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbf{II}\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}r_{a}r.

(84)

Bound on
\mathbf{III}
.
Define the following function class:

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{4}

\displaystyle=\Big\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)(X^{\prime}u)(X^{\prime}w),

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\>u\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\>\|u\|_{0}\leq s_{v},\>\|u\|_{2}\leq 1,\>w\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c})\cap\partial B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\Big\}.

By the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}\leq n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{3}}+r\frac{2\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{4}}.

Obviously, upper bounding the supremum over the set
\{v_{0}(\tau):\tau\in\mathcal{T}\}
by the supremum over the set of all
s
-sparse vectors is wasteful. However, in the present case, doing so does not change the asymptotic rate. (We derive a tighter bound on a similar quantity in the proof of Corollary
2
, where the tighter bound makes a difference.)

By eq. (
81
), Lemma
28
, and Assumptions
6
and
11
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\>r_{a}r.

and

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\>r,

Combine the preceding two upper bounds and the fact that
0\leq r_{a}<1/2
to conclude that, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\>r.\end{split}

(85)

Bound on
\mathbf{IV}
.
By eq. (
81
), Lemma
28
, and Assumptions
6
and
11
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})^{2}\bar{f}^{2}(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\>r^{2}.\end{split}

(86)

Bound on
\mathbf{V}
.
By eq. (
81
), Lemma
28
, and Assumptions
6
and
11
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{3}}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\>r_{a}r.\end{split}

(87)

Bound on
\mathbf{VI}
.

We introduce the following additional function classes:

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{5}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)(X^{\prime}u)^{2},\>u\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1)\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{6}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)(X^{\prime}u)^{2},\>u\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1)\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{7}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)(X^{\prime}u)^{2},\>u\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\>\|u\|_{2}\leq 1,\>\|u\|_{0}\leq s_{v},\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\}.

Then, by the triangle inequality, the bound on term
\mathbf{II}
, and the fact that
0\leq r_{a}<1/2
,

\displaystyle r_{f}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{3}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}+r_{f}r\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{4}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{3}|}+r_{f}r\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{4}|}+(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}r_{f}r

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}r_{a}r\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{5}}+r_{f}r\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}n^{-1/2}\Big(\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{6}}+\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{7}}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}r_{f}r.

Thus, by Lemma
28
and Assumptions
6
and
11
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}r_{f}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}r_{f}r+(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\>r_{f}r.\end{split}

(88)

Bound on
\mathbf{VII}
.

By Lemma
28
and Assumptions
6
and
11
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle r_{f}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{2}|}+(2+2\bar{c})^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})r_{f}r^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}+(2+2\bar{c})^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})r_{f}r^{2}

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})r_{f}r^{2}+(2+2\bar{c})^{2}\bar{f}^{2}(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\>r_{f}r^{2}.\end{split}

(89)

Bound on
\mathbf{VIII}
.
By Lemma
28
and Assumptions
6
and
11
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle r_{f}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{3}}\|g\|_{P_{n},1}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{3}|}+(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}r_{f}r_{a}r

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}r_{a}r\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}n^{-1/2}\Big(\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{5}}+\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{6}}\Big)+(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}r_{f}r_{a}r

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}r_{f}r_{a}r+(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\>r_{f}r_{a}r.\end{split}

(90)

Bound on
\mathbf{IX}
.
By the reverse triangle inequality and Lemma
24
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{v\in K(r,\tau)}\frac{\gamma}{n}\big(\|v\|_{1}-\|\tilde{v}(\tau)\|_{1}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\leq\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{v\in K(r,\tau)}\frac{\gamma}{n}\left(\sum_{k\in T_{v}(\tau)}\big(|v_{k}|-|\tilde{v}_{k}(\tau)|\big)+\sum_{k\in T_{v}^{c}(\tau)}|v_{k}|\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\leq\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{v\in K(r,\tau)}\frac{\gamma}{n}\left(\sum_{k\in T_{v}(\tau)}|v_{k}-\tilde{v}_{k}(\tau)|+\sum_{k\in T_{v}^{c}(\tau)}|v_{k}|\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\leq\frac{\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\gamma}{n}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}+(1+\bar{c})\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{v\in K(r,\tau)}\frac{\gamma}{n}\sum_{k\in T_{v}(\tau)}|v_{k}-\tilde{v}_{k}(\tau)|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\lesssim\frac{\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{n}+(1+\bar{c})\frac{\gamma}{n}s_{v}^{1/2}r.

(91)

Conclusion.
Combine Assumptions
6
and
11
, eq. (
83
)–(
I.4
), and observe that there exist absolute constants
c_{2},\ldots,c_{7}>0
such that, with probability at least
1-\eta-\delta
, the expression in eq. (
I.4
) can be lower bounded (up to a multiplicative constant) by

\displaystyle\kappa_{2}(2\bar{c})r^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}-c_{2}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}\big(r_{a}+r_{f}\big)r

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}-c_{3}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\frac{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|z\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\>r

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}-c_{4}(2+2\bar{c})^{2}\bar{f}^{2}(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\>r^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}-c_{5}(2+2\bar{c})^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})r_{f}r^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}-c_{6}\frac{\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{n}-c_{6}(1+\bar{c})\frac{\gamma}{n}s_{v}^{1/2}r

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}>0,

whenever

\displaystyle r

\displaystyle\geq c_{7}C\left(\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\vee 1\right)\left(\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\vee r_{f}\vee r_{a}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\bigvee c_{7}\left(\frac{\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\right)\left(\frac{\mu}{n}\vee\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\bigvee c_{7}\left(\frac{\bar{c}}{\kappa_{2}(2\bar{c})}\right)\frac{\gamma\sqrt{s_{v}}}{n}.

where
C=(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\bar{c}^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})/\kappa_{2}(2\bar{c})
. (Note that
c_{7}>0
can be chosen such that
r>\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}
.) To conclude, adjust constants.

∎

Proof of Corollary
2
.

The proof consists of two parts. First, we derive an upper bound on the gradient of the objective function. Then, we derive an upper bound on the empirical covariance matrix. Any
\gamma,\mu>0
grater than or equal to these upper bounds will satisfy eq. (
39
) and (
40
). To simplify notation, we write
v_{0}(\tau)
,
\hat{v}(\tau)
,
T_{v}(\tau)
, and
s_{v}
for
v_{0}(\tau;z)
,
\hat{v}(\tau;z)
,
T_{v}(\tau;z)
, and
s_{v}(z)
, respectively. Let
\zeta\in(0,1)
be arbitrary and
\mathcal{T}_{\zeta}
be an
\zeta
-net with cardinality
\mathrm{card}(T_{\zeta})\leq 1+1/\zeta
.

First part.

By the definition of
v_{0}(\tau)
and repeated applications of the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)+nz\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\leq\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(f_{i}^{2}(\tau)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}-\mathbb{E}[f_{i}^{2}(\tau)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}]\big)v_{0}(\tau)\right\|_{\infty}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)-f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\leq\sup_{s\in T_{\zeta}}\sup_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(f_{i}^{2}(s^{\prime})X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}-\mathbb{E}[f_{i}^{2}(s^{\prime})X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}]\big)v_{0}(s)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{s\in T_{\zeta}}\sup_{\tau:|\tau-s|\leq\zeta}\sup_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(f_{i}^{2}(s^{\prime})X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}-\mathbb{E}[f_{i}^{2}(s^{\prime})X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}]\big)\big(v_{0}(\tau)-v_{0}(s)\big)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{s\in T_{\zeta}}\sup_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(s^{\prime})-f_{i}^{2}(s^{\prime})\big)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(s)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{s\in T_{\zeta}}\sup_{\tau:|\tau-s|\leq\zeta}\sup_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(s^{\prime})-f_{i}^{2}(s^{\prime})\big)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}\big(v_{0}(\tau)-v_{0}(s)\big)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}=\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{II}+\mathbf{III}+\mathbf{IV}.

(92)

We now bound the four terms on the far right hand side in above display.

Bound on
\mathbf{I}
.
By Lemma
28
, Assumptions
6
and
11
, and the union bound over
s\in\mathcal{T}_{\zeta}
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}\lesssim\bar{c}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(1)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\log p+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1+1/\zeta)+\log(1/\delta)}.

(93)

Bound on
\mathbf{II}
.
By Lemmas
25
and
28
, Assumptions
6
and
11
, and the union bound over
s\in\mathcal{T}_{\zeta}
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{II}&\lesssim\bar{c}\bar{f}^{3}L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{v})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(1)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}(p)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{n}\sqrt{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+\log p+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1+1/\zeta)+\log(1/\delta)}\>\zeta.\end{split}

(94)

Bound on
\mathbf{III}
.
We introduce the following two function classes:

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{1}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(s^{\prime})|X)X_{k}^{2},\>1\leq k\leq p,\>s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(s^{\prime})|X)(X^{\prime}v)^{2},\>v\in\big\{v_{0}(s)\kappa_{2}(\infty)/\|z\|_{2}:s\in T_{\zeta}\big\},\>s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}\}.

Recall that by eq. (
80
), with probability at least
1-\eta
, we have
|\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)-f_{i}^{2}(\tau)|\lesssim r_{f}f_{i}^{2}(\tau).
Thus, with probability at least
1-\eta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{III}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}\sup_{s\in T_{\zeta}}\sup_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}}\max_{1\leq k\leq p}\left(\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(s^{\prime})\big)|X_{ik}||X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(s)|\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}\sqrt{n}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\big(\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}+\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}\big)+r_{f}n\>\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}.

Therefore, by Lemma
28
, Assumptions
6
and
11
, and the union bound over
s\in\mathcal{T}_{\zeta}
, we have, with probability at least
1-\eta-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{III}&\lesssim\bar{c}^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\log p+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1+1/\zeta)+\log(1/\delta)}\>r_{f}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\bar{c}^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\>nr_{f}.\end{split}

(95)

Bound on
\mathbf{IV}
.
Analogous to the bounds of
\mathbf{II}
and
\mathbf{III}
, we have, with probability at least
1-\eta-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{IV}&\lesssim\bar{c}^{2}\bar{f}^{3}L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}(2s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}(p)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{n}\sqrt{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+\log p+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1+1/\zeta)+\log(1/\delta)}\>r_{f}\>\zeta\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\bar{c}^{2}\bar{f}^{3}L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}(2s_{v})\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}(p)\>nr_{f}\>\zeta.\end{split}

(96)

Since
\zeta\in(0,1)
is arbitrary, we can choose
\zeta\asymp 1/\big(L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}(p)\sqrt{p}\big)
. Combine the bounds in eq. (
I.4
)–(
96
), adjust some constants, and conclude that with probability at least
1-\eta-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)+nz\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{c}^{2}\bar{f}^{3}\varphi_{\max}(2s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\left(\sqrt{\log(np/\delta)+\log\big(L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}(p)\big)}+\sqrt{n}r_{f}\right)\sqrt{n}.

We further upper bound (and simplify) the term on the right hand side in above display. Recall from Theorem
5

C_{3}=\bar{c}^{2}\bar{f}^{2}L_{f}L_{\theta}\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})/\kappa_{2}(\bar{c})
. Then,

\displaystyle\bar{c}^{2}\bar{f}^{3}\varphi_{\max}(2s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\left(\sqrt{\log(np/\delta)+\log\big(L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}(p)\big)}+\sqrt{n}r_{f}\right)\sqrt{n}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\lesssim}\bar{c}^{2}\bar{f}^{3}L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}(2s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}(2s_{\theta})\big)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\left(\sqrt{\log(np/\delta)}+\sqrt{n}r_{f}\right)\sqrt{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{3}\bar{f}\frac{\varphi_{\max}(2s_{v})}{\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})}\frac{\kappa_{2}(\bar{c})\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\left(\sqrt{\log(np/\delta)}+\sqrt{n}r_{f}\right)\sqrt{n}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\lesssim}C_{3}\bar{f}\frac{\kappa_{2}(\bar{c})\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\left(\sqrt{\log(np/\delta)}+\sqrt{n}r_{f}\right)\sqrt{n},

where (a) and (b) follow from Lemma 13 in
Belloni and Chernozhukov, 2011
(more specifically, (a) holds since
\log\big(\varphi_{\max}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}(p)\big)\lesssim 2\log\big(\varphi_{\max}(2s_{\theta})\big)+\log p
by Lemma 13 in
Belloni and Chernozhukov, 2011
and (b) holds since
\varphi_{\max}(2s_{v})/\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})\leq 2
and
\varphi_{\max}(1)/\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})\leq 1
.)

Therefore, there exists an absolute constant
C_{\gamma}>0
such that for all

\displaystyle\gamma\geq C_{\gamma}c_{0}C_{3}\bar{f}\frac{\kappa_{2}(\bar{c})\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\left(\sqrt{\log(np/\delta)}+\sqrt{n}r_{f}\right)\sqrt{n},

(97)

eq. (
39
) holds with probability at least
1-\eta-\delta
.

Second part.

Next, by the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{u\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau;z),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,r_{a})}\left|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\leq\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{u\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau;z),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,r_{a})}\left|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)-f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\big)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{u\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau;z),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,r_{a})}\left|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}-\mathbb{E}\big[f_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}\big]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{u\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau;z),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,r_{a})}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}\right].

Recall eq.
80
and upper bound the right hand side in above inequality (up to a multiplicative constant), with probability at least
1-\eta
, by

\displaystyle(1+r_{f})\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{u\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau;z),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,r_{a})}\left|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}-\mathbb{E}\big[f_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}\big]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+(1+r_{f})\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{u\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau;z),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,r_{a})}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)^{2}\right].

By Lemma
28
, Assumptions
6
and
11
, we upper bound these two terms (up to a multiplicative constant), with probability at least
1-\eta-\delta
, by

\displaystyle\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\sqrt{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+\log(1/\delta)}\sqrt{n}r_{a}^{2}+\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})nr_{a}^{2}.

Therefore, there exists an absolute constant
C_{\mu}>0
such that for all

\displaystyle\mu\geq C_{\mu}c_{0}\left(\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\sqrt{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+\log(1/\delta)}\sqrt{n}r_{a}^{2}+\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})nr_{a}^{2}\right),

(98)

eq. (
40
) holds with probability at least
1-\eta-\delta
.

Conclusion.
Plug the lower bounds in eq. (
97
) and (
98
) into the rate
r>0
from Theorem
5
and simplify the expression using Assumptions
6
and
11
to conclude that with probability at least
1-\eta-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left\|\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\lesssim C_{3}\left(\frac{C_{\gamma}\bar{c}\bar{f}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\vee\frac{C_{\mu}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{C_{\gamma}\bar{c}\bar{f}\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\vee\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\vee 1\right)\left(\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(np/\delta)+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})}{n}}\vee r_{f}\sqrt{s_{v}}\vee r_{a}\vee\frac{r_{a}^{2}}{r_{f}}\right),

where
C_{3}=(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\bar{c}^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})/\kappa_{2}(2\bar{c})
. ∎

I.5
Proofs of Section
G.4

Proof of Lemma
19
.

To simplify notation we write
f_{i}(\tau)
for
f_{Y|X}(X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X_{i})
. We have

\displaystyle\left|\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-f_{i}(\tau)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|1/\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-1/f_{i}(\tau)\right|\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)f_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle\leq\left|1/\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-1/f_{i}(\tau)\right|\left|\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-f_{i}(\tau)\right|f_{i}(\tau)+\left|1/\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-1/f_{i}(\tau)\right|f_{i}^{2}(\tau).

If
\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\max_{1\leq i\leq n}|1/\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-1/f_{i}(\tau)|f_{i}(\tau)<1/2
, this can be rearranged to yield

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}{f_{i}(\tau)}-1\right|\leq 2\bar{f}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{1}{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}-\frac{1}{f_{i}(\tau)}\right|.

(99)

Thus, the claim of the lemma follows if
\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\max_{1\leq i\leq n}|1/\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-1/f_{i}(\tau)|\rightarrow 0
at the prescribed rate.

Let
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
and
h>0
be arbitrary. Two third-order Taylor expansions of the CQF give

\displaystyle Q_{Y}(\tau+h;X)

\displaystyle=Q_{Y}(\tau;X)+Q_{Y}^{\prime}(\tau;X)h+Q_{Y}^{\prime\prime}(\tau;X)h^{2}/2+Q_{Y}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{+};X)h^{3}/6,

\displaystyle Q_{Y}(\tau-h;X)

\displaystyle=Q_{Y}(\tau;X)-Q_{Y}^{\prime}(\tau;X)h+Q_{Y}^{\prime\prime}(\tau;X)h^{2}/2-Q_{Y}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{-};X)h^{3}/6,

where
\zeta_{+}\in(\tau,\tau+h)
and
\zeta_{-}\in(\tau-h,\tau)
. Combine both expansions and conclude that

\displaystyle Q_{Y}^{\prime}(\tau;X)=\frac{Q_{Y}(\tau+h;X)-Q_{Y}(\tau-h;X)}{2h}+\left(Q_{Y}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{+};X)-Q_{Y}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{-};X)\right)h^{2}/12.

(100)

Recall the identity
Q_{Y}^{\prime}(\tau;X)=1/f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)
and invoke Assumption
13
to arrive at

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{1}{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}-\frac{1}{f_{i}(\tau)}\right|\\
&\leq\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{X_{i}^{\prime}\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau+h)-X_{i}^{\prime}\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau-h)-Q(\tau+h;X_{i})+Q(\tau-h;X_{i})}{2h}\right|+C_{Q}h^{2}.\end{split}

(101)

By Assumption
1
, Lemma
11
and
12
,

\displaystyle\big\|\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{u\in C^{p}(T_{\theta}(\tau),\bar{c})\cap B^{p}(0,1)}X_{i}^{\prime}u\big\|_{\psi_{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\log n+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta}),

and by Theorem
3
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\|\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau)-\theta_{0}(\tau)\|_{2}\lesssim\left(\frac{\bar{c}\phi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})L_{\theta}}{\kappa_{1}(\bar{c})}\vee 1\right)\sqrt{\frac{s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log n+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\bigvee\frac{\bar{c}}{\kappa_{1}(\bar{c})}\frac{\lambda\sqrt{s_{\theta}}}{n}=:r_{\theta}.

Hence, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{1}{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}-\frac{1}{f_{i}(\tau)}\right|\lesssim\sqrt{n}h^{-1}r_{\theta}^{2}+C_{Q}h^{2}.

By assumption the right hand side in above display vanishes as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
. Combine this with eq. (
99
) to conclude the proof.
∎

Proof of Theorem
6
.

To simplify notation, we write
f_{i}(\tau)
,
\tilde{v}(\tau)
,
v_{0}(\tau)
, and
T_{v}(\tau)
instead of
f_{Y|X}(X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X_{i})
,
\tilde{v}(\tau;z)
,
v_{0}(\tau;z)
, and
T_{v}(\tau;z)
, respectively. Note that
\hat{a}\hat{b}\hat{c}-abc=(\hat{a}-a)(\hat{b}-b)(\hat{c}-c)+(\hat{a}-a)(\hat{b}-b)c+(\hat{a}-a)b(\hat{c}-c)+a(\hat{b}-b)(\hat{c}-c)+(\hat{a}-a)bc+a(\hat{b}-b)c+ab(\hat{c}-c)
for arbitrary
\hat{a},\hat{b},\hat{c},a,b,c\in\mathbb{R}
. Hence, the error term can be expanded in the following way:

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}|e_{n}(\tau;z)|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}=\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left|z^{\prime}\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau)-z^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)-\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)\big(\tau-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau)\}\big)X_{i}^{\prime}\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\left.+\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}(\tau)\big(\tau-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\big)X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\leq\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}(\tau)\big(\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau)\}-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\big)X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)+z^{\prime}\big(\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau)-\theta_{0}(\tau)\big)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-f_{i}(\tau)\big)\big(\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau)\}\big)X_{i}^{\prime}\big(\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}(\tau)\big(\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau)\}\big)X_{i}^{\prime}\big(\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-f_{i}(\tau)\big)\big(\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau)\}\big)X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-f_{i}(\tau)\big)\big(\tau-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\big)X_{i}^{\prime}\big(\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}(\tau)\big(\tau-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\big)X_{i}^{\prime}\big(\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-f_{i}(\tau)\big)\big(\tau-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\big)X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}=\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{II}+\mathbf{III}+\mathbf{IV}+\mathbf{V}+\mathbf{VI}+\mathbf{VII}.

(102)

Unless
v_{0}(\tau)
is sparse it is impossible to directly bound the difference between
\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)
and to control the supremum of
a^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)
uniformly in
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
and arbitrary
a\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
tightly enough to allow for
p\gg n
. To get around this problem, we will add and subtract the sparse approximation
\tilde{v}(\tau)
in each of the terms
\mathbf{I}
–
\mathbf{VII}
. By construction of
\tilde{v}(\tau)
, we can then bound the differences
\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)
and
\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)
and control the supremum of
a^{\prime}\tilde{v}(\tau)
uniformly in
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
and arbitrary
a\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
. In particular, we have, by Lemmas
23
(i) and
24
,

\displaystyle v_{0}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)

\displaystyle\in\left\{u\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:(u_{T_{v}(\tau)},u_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)})=(0,w_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)}),\>w\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\right\},

(103)

\displaystyle\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)

\displaystyle\in C_{1}^{p}(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c})\cup B^{p}_{1}\left(0,\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right),

(104)

and, by Definition
10
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\|v_{0}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)\|_{2}\lesssim\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}r_{a},

(105)

\displaystyle\tilde{v}(\tau)\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}\left(0,\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right).

(106)

We are now ready to bound the seven terms on the far right hand side in above display (
I.5
).

Bound on
\mathbf{I}
.
Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{1}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big(1\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-1\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta\}\big)X^{\prime}v,\>\>\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{p},

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\|\theta\|_{0}\leq n,\>\|\theta-\theta_{0}(\tau)\|_{2}\leq r_{\theta},\>v\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big(1\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-1\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta\}\big)X^{\prime}v,\>\>\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{p},

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\|\theta\|_{0}\leq n,\>\|\theta-\theta_{0}(\tau)\|_{2}\leq r_{\theta},\>v\in U^{p}\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

where
U^{p}:=\left\{u\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:(u_{T_{v}(\tau)},u_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)})=(0,w_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)}),\>w\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\right\}
. Every
g\in\mathcal{G}_{q}
is uniquely determined by a triplet
(v,\theta,\tau)
; hence, we may write
g=g_{v,\theta,\tau}
Let
\hat{s}_{\lambda}:=\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\|\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau)\|_{2}
and define
\mathcal{G}_{q}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})=\{g_{v,\theta,\tau}\in\mathcal{G}_{q}:\|\theta\|_{0}\leq\hat{s}_{\lambda}\}
,
q\in\{1,2\}
. Thus, by the geometric constraints (
103
), (
105
), and (
106
), and Theorem
3
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})}+\frac{r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{\theta}\left|\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\big[X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big(1\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta\}-1\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\big)\big]+z^{\prime}\theta-z^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\right|

\displaystyle\equiv\mathbf{I}_{1}+\mathbf{I}_{2}+\mathbf{I}_{3},

where the supremum in
\theta
is taken over
\|\theta\|_{0}\leq\hat{s}_{\lambda}
and
\|\theta-\theta_{0}(\tau)\|_{2}\leq r_{\theta}
.

By Lemma
31
(ii), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{I}_{1}&\lesssim\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\big(\upsilon_{r_{\theta},n}\big(\hat{s}_{\lambda}\log(1/r_{\theta})\big)+\upsilon_{r_{\theta},n}(t_{s_{v},\hat{s}_{\lambda},s_{\theta},n,\delta})\big),\end{split}

(107)

and, by Lemma
31
(ii) combined with Lemma
23
(ii), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{I}_{2}&\lesssim\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\frac{r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\big(\upsilon_{r_{\theta},n}\big(\hat{s}_{\lambda}\log(1/r_{\theta})\big)+\upsilon_{r_{\theta},n}(t_{s_{v},\hat{s}_{\lambda},s_{\theta},n,\delta})\big),\end{split}

(108)

where
t_{s_{v},\hat{s}_{\lambda},s_{\theta},n,\delta}=s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+\hat{s}_{\lambda}\log(ep/\hat{s}_{\lambda})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(L_{f}L_{\theta}n/\delta)
and
\upsilon_{r_{\theta},n}(z)=\sqrt{z}\big(\sqrt{r_{\theta}}+n^{-1/2}(\log n)\sqrt{z}+n^{-1}(\log n)^{3/2}z\big)
for
z\geq 0
.

By a first-order Taylor approximation of
F_{Y|X}
in
X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)
(with Peano’s remainder term) and by the definition of
v_{0}(\tau)
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{\theta}\left|\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\big[X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)X^{\prime}\big(\theta-\theta_{0}(\tau)\big)+z^{\prime}\theta-z^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{\theta}\left|\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\big[X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)L_{f}\big(X^{\prime}(\theta-\theta_{0}(\tau))\big)^{2}\big]\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{L_{f}}{2}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{\theta}\left|\mathbb{E}\big[X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big(X^{\prime}(\theta-\theta_{0}(\tau))\big)^{2}\big]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{f}L_{f}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}r_{\theta}^{2}.

(109)

Combine eq. (
107
)–(
I.5
), use that
(s_{v}+s_{\theta})^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
, and conclude that, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{I}&\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{\theta}^{2}\right).\end{split}

(110)

Bound on
\mathbf{II}
.
Recall the function classes
\mathcal{G}_{1}
and
\mathcal{G}_{2}
. Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{3}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big(1\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-1\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta\}\big)(X^{\prime}v),\>\>\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{p},

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\|\theta\|_{0}\leq n,\>\|\theta-\theta_{0}(\tau)\|_{2}\leq r_{\theta},\>v\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\>\|v\|_{0}=1,\>\|v\|_{1}=1,\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

Every
g\in\mathcal{G}_{3}
is uniquely determined by atriplet
(v,\theta,\tau)
; hence, we may write
g=g_{v,\theta,\tau}
. Define
\mathcal{G}_{3}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})=\{g_{v,\theta,\tau}\in\mathcal{G}_{q}:\|\theta\|_{0}\leq\hat{s}_{\lambda}\}
.

Using the geometric constraints (
103
), (
104
), and (
105
), Theorems
3
and
5
, and Lemma
19
with
r_{f}=o(1)
followed by Lemmas
11
and
12
, we have, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{II}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}r_{v}\|P_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{1}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}+r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\|P_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{3}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|P_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}\equiv\mathbf{II}_{1}+\mathbf{II}_{2}+\mathbf{II}_{3}.

Bound on
\mathbf{II}_{1}
.
Since
(s_{v}+s_{\theta})^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
we have, by Lemma
31
(iii), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{II}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}r_{v}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{1}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}+r_{f}r_{v}\sup_{\tau}\sup_{\theta}\sup_{v}\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big|\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta\}\big||X^{\prime}v|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}r_{v}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{1}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}+L_{f}\bar{f}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta}+\hat{s}_{\lambda})r_{f}r_{v}r_{\theta}

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\frac{r_{f}r_{v}}{\sqrt{n}}\big(\upsilon_{r_{\theta},n}\big(\hat{s}_{\lambda}\log(1/r_{\theta})\big)+\upsilon_{r_{\theta},n}(t_{s_{v},\hat{s}_{\lambda},s_{\theta},n,\delta})+\sqrt{n}r_{\theta}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)\times r_{f}r_{v}\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{\theta}\right),

(111)

where the supremum in the first line is taken over
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
,
v\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1)
, and
\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
such that
\|\theta\|_{0}\leq\hat{s}_{\lambda}
and
\|\theta-\theta_{0}(\tau)\|_{2}\leq r_{\theta}
and the expected value can be bounded by the term in the second line
|\mathbf{1}_{A}-\mathbf{1}_{B}|=\mathbf{1}_{A\setminus B}+\mathbf{1}_{B\setminus A}
for arbitrary sets
A,B
(see also proof of Lemma
31
).

Bound on
\mathbf{II}_{2}
.
As above, since
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
we have, by Lemma
31
(iv), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{II}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{3}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\sup_{\tau}\sup_{\theta}\sup_{v}\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big|\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta\}\big||X^{\prime}v|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{3}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}+L_{f}\bar{f}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(1)\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta}+\hat{s}_{\lambda})r_{f}r_{\theta}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(1)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\frac{r_{f}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\big(\upsilon_{r_{\theta},n}\big(\hat{s}_{\lambda}\log(1/r_{\theta})\big)+\upsilon_{r_{\theta},n}(t_{1,\hat{s}_{\lambda},s_{\theta},n,\delta})+\sqrt{n}r_{\theta}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(1)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)\times r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{\theta}\right),

(112)

where the supremum in the first line is taken over
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
,
v\in B^{p}_{1}(0,1)
, and
\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
such that
\|\theta\|_{0}\leq\hat{s}_{\lambda}
and
\|\theta-\theta_{0}(\tau)\|_{2}\leq r_{\theta}
. (This bound is loose, but a tighter bound on
\mathbf{II}_{2}
does not result in a tighter over all bound because the upper bound on
\mathbf{II}_{1}
is of the same order.)

Bound on
\mathbf{II}_{3}
.
Again, since
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
we have, by Lemma
31
(iii) combined with Lemma
23
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{II}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sup_{\tau}\sup_{\theta}\sup_{v}\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big|\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta\}\big||X^{\prime}v|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}+L_{f}\bar{f}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(1)\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta}+\hat{s}_{\lambda})r_{f}r_{a}r_{\theta}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\frac{r_{f}r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\big(\upsilon_{r_{\theta},n}\big(\hat{s}_{\lambda}\log(1/r_{\theta})\big)+\upsilon_{r_{\theta},n}(t_{s_{v},\hat{s}_{\lambda},s_{\theta},n,\delta})+\sqrt{n}r_{\theta}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)\times r_{f}r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{\theta}\right),

(113)

where the supremum in the first line is taken over
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
,
v\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1)
(by Lemma
23
(ii)), and
\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
such that
\|\theta\|_{0}\leq\hat{s}_{\lambda}
and
\|\theta-\theta_{0}(\tau)\|_{2}\leq r_{\theta}
.

Combine the bounds (
I.5
)–(
I.5
) to conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{II}&\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\left(r_{f}r_{v}+r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{\theta}\right).\end{split}

(114)

Bound on
\mathbf{III}
.
Recall the definition of the function classes
\mathcal{G}_{1},\mathcal{G}_{2},\mathcal{G}_{3}
. Then, by the geometric constraints (
103
), (
104
), and (
105
), Theorems
3
and
5
, and Lemma
19
with
r_{f}=o(1)
followed by Lemmas
11
and

, we have, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{III}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{v}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})}+\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{3}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})}+r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})}\equiv\mathbf{III}_{1}+\mathbf{III}_{2}+\mathbf{III}_{3}.

We can bound
\mathbf{III}_{1},\mathbf{III}_{2},\mathbf{III}_{3}
using the same arguments as those used to bound
\mathbf{II}_{1},\mathbf{II}_{2},\mathbf{II}_{3}
. The only difference is that we apply Lemma
31
(iv) instead of (iii). Thus, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{III}&\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\left(r_{v}+\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}+r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{\theta}\right).\end{split}

(115)

Bound on
\mathbf{IV}
.
Recall the definition of the function classes
\mathcal{G}_{1},\mathcal{G}_{2}
. By the geometric constraints (
103
), (
105
), and (
106
), Theorem
3
, and Lemma
19
with
r_{f}=o(1)
, we have, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{IV}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|P_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{1}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|P_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}\equiv\mathbf{IV}_{1}+\mathbf{IV}_{2}.

To bound
\mathbf{IV}_{1},\mathbf{IV}_{2}
we use Lemma
31
(iii): Recall that
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
. Then, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{IV}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{1}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sup_{\tau}\sup_{\theta}\sup_{v}\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big|\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta\}\big||X^{\prime}v|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)\times r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{\theta}\right),

and, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{IV}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda})|}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sup_{\tau}\sup_{\theta}\sup_{v}\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big|\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta\}\big||X^{\prime}v|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)\times r_{f}r_{a}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{\theta}\right).

Hence, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{IV}&\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\left(r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{\theta}\right).\end{split}

(116)

Bound on
\mathbf{V}
.
To derive a tight bound on this term we leverage the special structure of the estimates
\{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)\}_{i=1}^{n}
and properties of quantiles and associated densities: Note that

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}{f_{i}(\tau)}-1=\left(\frac{1}{f_{i}(\tau)}-\frac{1}{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}\right)\left(\frac{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}{f_{i}(\tau)}-1\right)f_{i}(\tau)+\left(\frac{1}{f_{i}(\tau)}-\frac{1}{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}\right)f_{i}(\tau).

Further, recall that

\displaystyle Q_{Y}^{\prime}(\tau;X)=\frac{1}{f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)},

and that by eq. (
100
) from the proof of Lemma
19
, for
h>0
arbitrary,

\displaystyle Q_{Y}^{\prime}(\tau;X)=\frac{Q_{Y}(\tau+h;X)-Q_{Y}(\tau-h;X)}{2h}+\left(Q_{Y}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{+};X)-Q_{Y}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{-};X)\right)h^{2}.

Combine above identities to conclude that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)-f_{i}(\tau)&=f_{i}^{2}(\tau)h^{2}\big(Q_{Y}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{+};X)-Q_{Y}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{-};X)\big)\left(\left(\frac{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}{f_{i}(\tau)}-1\right)+1\right)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}+f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\left(\frac{X_{i}^{\prime}\big(\theta_{0}(\tau+h)-\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau+h)\big)}{2h}\right)\left(\left(\frac{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}{f_{i}(\tau)}-1\right)+1\right)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}+f_{i}^{2}(\tau)\left(\frac{X_{i}^{\prime}\big(\theta_{0}(\tau-h)-\hat{\theta}_{\lambda}(\tau-h)\big)}{2h}\right)\left(\left(\frac{\hat{f}_{i}(\tau)}{f_{i}(\tau)}-1\right)+1\right).\end{split}

(117)

The remaining steps are similar to those that we have already developed. Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{4}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=(X^{\prime}u)(X^{\prime}v),\>v\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>u\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{\theta}(\tau),\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{5}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=(X^{\prime}u)(X^{\prime}v),\>v\in U^{p}\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>u\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{\theta}(\tau),\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{6}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=(X^{\prime}u)(X^{\prime}v),\>v\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\>\|v\|_{0}=1,\>\|v\|_{1}\leq 1,\>u\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{\theta}(\tau),\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{7}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big(\tau-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\big)(X^{\prime}u)(X^{\prime}v),

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}v\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>u\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{\theta}(\tau),\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{8}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big(\tau-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\big)(X^{\prime}u)(X^{\prime}v),

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}v\in U^{p}\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>u\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{\theta}(\tau),\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{9}

\displaystyle=\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big(\tau-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\big)(X^{\prime}u)(X^{\prime}v),

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}v\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\>\|v\|_{0}=1,\>\|v\|_{1}\leq 1,\>u\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{\theta}(\tau),\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\},

where
U^{p}:=\left\{u\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:(u_{T_{v}(\tau)},u_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)})=(0,w_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)}),\>w\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\right\}
.

Using the geometric constraints (
103
), (
104
), and (
105
), the expansion (
117
), Theorems
3
and
5
, and Lemma
19
with
r_{f}=o(1)
followed by Lemmas
11
and
12
, we have, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{f}^{2}(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})\left(r_{f}r_{v}\|P_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{4}|}+r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\|P_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{6}|}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|P_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{5}|}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})\left(r_{v}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{7}}+\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{9}}+r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{8}}\right)

\displaystyle\equiv(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})\left(\mathbf{V}_{1}+\mathbf{V}_{2}+\mathbf{V}_{3}+\mathbf{V}_{4}+\mathbf{V}_{5}+\mathbf{V}_{6}\right).

Recall that
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
. Whence, by Lemma
14
(ii), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\bar{f}^{2}\frac{r_{f}r_{v}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{4}|}+\bar{f}^{2}r_{f}r_{v}\sup_{v}\sup_{u}\mathbb{E}\left[|X^{\prime}u||X^{\prime}v|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\times\left(r_{f}r_{v}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{f}r_{v}\right),

by Lemma

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bar{f}^{2}2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{6}|}+r_{f}\frac{\bar{f}^{2}2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\sup_{v}\sup_{u}\mathbb{E}\left[|X^{\prime}u||X^{\prime}v|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\times\left(r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right),

and by Lemma
14
(iii), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bar{f}^{2}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{5}|}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{\bar{f}^{2}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sup_{v}\sup_{u}\mathbb{E}\left[|X^{\prime}u||X^{\prime}v|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\times\left(r_{f}r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right).

By Lemma
30
(i), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{4}=\frac{r_{v}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{7}}\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\times r_{v}\hat{r}_{B},

by Lemma
30
(i), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{5}=\frac{2\bar{c}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{9}}\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\times\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\hat{r}_{B},

and by Lemma

(ii), with probability at least

,

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{6}=\frac{r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{8}}\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\times r_{a}\frac{\bar{f}^{2}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\hat{r}_{B}.

Combine these bounds with

and conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{V}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})\left(r_{v}+\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}+r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)\hat{r}_{B}.\end{split}

(118)

Bound on
\mathbf{VI}
.
Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{10}

\displaystyle=\left\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\big\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\right)X^{\prime}v,\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\left.v\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{11}

\displaystyle=\left\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\big\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\right)X^{\prime}v,\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\left.v\in U^{p}\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{12}

\displaystyle=\left\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\big\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\right)X^{\prime}v,\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\left.\>v\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\>\|v\|_{0}=1,\>\|v\|_{1}\leq 1,\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\right\},

where
U^{p}:=\left\{u\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:(u_{T_{v}(\tau)},u_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)})=(0,w_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)}),\>w\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\right\}
.

Using the geometric constraints (
103
), (

), and (

), Theorems
3
and
5
followed by Lemmas
11
and

, we have, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{VI}\lesssim r_{v}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{10}}+\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{12}}+r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{11}}\equiv\mathbf{VI}_{1}+\mathbf{VI}_{2}+\mathbf{VI}_{3}.

Recall that
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
. Whence, by Lemma
29
(i), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{VI}_{1}=\frac{r_{v}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{10}}\lesssim\bar{f}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\times r_{v}\hat{r}_{B},

by Lemma

, with probability at least

,

\displaystyle\mathbf{VI}_{2}=\frac{2\bar{c}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{12}}\lesssim\bar{f}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\times\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\hat{r}_{B},

,

Combine these bounds to conclude that with probability at least

,

\displaystyle\mathbf{VI}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{f}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\times\left(r_{v}+\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}+r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)\hat{r}_{B}.

(119)

Bound on
\mathbf{VII}.
Recall the function classes
\mathcal{G}_{4},\mathcal{G}_{5},\mathcal{G}_{7}
, and
\mathcal{G}_{8}
. By the geometric constraints (

), (

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{f}^{2}(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})\left(r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|P_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{4}|}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|P_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{5}|}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{7}}+r_{a}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{8}}\right)

\displaystyle\equiv(1+r_{f})(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})\left(\mathbf{VII}_{1}+\mathbf{VII}_{2}+\mathbf{VII}_{3}+\mathbf{VII}_{4}\right).

Since
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
we have by Lemma
14
(ii), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{VII}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bar{f}^{2}2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{4}|}+r_{f}\frac{\bar{f}^{2}2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sup_{v}\sup_{u}\mathbb{E}\left[|X^{\prime}u||X^{\prime}v|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\times\left(r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right),

and by Lemma
