(ii), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{VII}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{f}r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bar{f}^{2}2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{|\mathcal{G}_{5}|}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{\bar{f}^{2}2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sup_{v}\sup_{u}\mathbb{E}\left[|X^{\prime}u||X^{\prime}v|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\times\left(r_{f}r_{a}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{f}r_{a}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right).

By Lemma
30
(i), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{VII}_{3}=\frac{2\bar{c}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{7}}\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\times\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\hat{r}_{B},

and Lemma
30
(i), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{VII}_{4}=\frac{r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{8}}\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\times r_{a}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\hat{r}_{B}.

Since
r_{f}\vee r_{a}=o(1)
, we conclude that, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{VII}\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\times(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})\left(r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\hat{r}_{B}\right).

(120)

Conclusion.
Since
r_{f}\vee r_{a}=o(1)
and by definition of
r_{v}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{II}+\mathbf{III}+\mathbf{IV}

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}\right)\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times r_{\theta}\left(r_{v}+r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+r_{\theta}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\left[\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}+r_{\theta}\right)r_{v}+\left(\hat{r}_{B}^{2}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\hat{r}_{B}\right)\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right],

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}+\mathbf{VII}

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})\left(r_{f}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\hat{r}_{B}+\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\hat{r}_{B}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\times(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})\left[r_{v}\hat{r}_{B}+\hat{r}_{B}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right],

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{VI}\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\times(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})r_{v}\hat{r}_{B}.

Combine these bounds with
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
and conclude that

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}|e_{n}(\tau;z)|

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{4}\left(\hat{r}_{B}(\log n)+\sqrt{r_{\theta}}\right)\left(r_{v}\hat{r}_{B}+\hat{r}_{B}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)+C_{4}r_{\theta}r_{v}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+C_{5}(C_{Q}h^{2}+h^{-1}r_{\theta})\left(r_{v}\hat{r}_{B}+\hat{r}_{B}\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right),

where

\displaystyle\begin{split}C_{4}&:=L_{f}\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(\hat{s}_{\lambda}+s_{\theta})\big),\\
C_{5}&:=(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big).\end{split}

(121)

∎

Proof of Corollary
3
.

The claim follows by combining Theorem
6
with Theorem
4
(ii) and Corollary
2
. The rate on the remainder term can be simplified by exploiting that, by assumption,
(s_{v}+s_{\theta})^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(nh^{2})
and
h^{2}s_{v}=o(1)
.
∎

I.6
Proofs of Section
G.5

Proof of Lemma
20
.

To simplify notation, we write
f_{i}(\tau)
,
v_{0}(\tau)
, and
T_{v}(\tau)
instead of
f_{Y|X}(X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X_{i})
,
v_{0}(\tau;z)
, and
T_{v}(\tau;z)
, respectively.

Proof of (i).
Let
q\geq 1
be arbitrary.
For all
g_{\tau},g_{\tau^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{G}

\displaystyle\left\|(g_{\tau}-Pg_{\tau})-(g_{\tau^{\prime}}-Pg_{\tau^{\prime}})\right\|_{P,q}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}=\left\|g_{\tau}-g_{\tau^{\prime}}\right\|_{P,q}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\leq\left\|\big(f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)-f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})|X)\big)\big(\tau-\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\big)X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\right\|_{P,q}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\left\|f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big(\tau-\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\big)X^{\prime}\big(v_{0}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\big)\right\|_{P,q}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\left\|f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})|X)X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\right\|_{P,q}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\left\|f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})|X)\big(\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\}\big)X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\right\|_{P,q}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{II}+\mathbf{III}+\mathbf{IV}.

(122)

Bound on
\mathbf{I}
.
By Assumption
5
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\big(f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)-f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})|X)\big)X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\right\|_{P,q}

\displaystyle\leq L_{f}\left\|\big(\theta_{0}(\tau)-\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\big)XX^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\right\|_{P,q}

\displaystyle\leq L_{f}\|v_{0}(\tau)\|_{2}\|\theta_{0}(\tau)-\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\|_{2}\sup_{u,w}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[|X^{\prime}u|^{q}|X^{\prime}w|^{q}\right]\right)^{1/q},

where the supremum over is taken over all
u,w
satisfying
\|u\|_{2}=\|w\|_{2}=1
,
\|u\|_{0}\leq s_{v}
and
\|w\|_{0}\leq 2s_{\theta}
. By Assumption
1
we can upper bound the expected values in the last displayed line as follows:

\displaystyle\sup_{u,w}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[|X^{\prime}u|^{q}|X^{\prime}w|^{q}\right]\right)^{1/q}\leq\sup_{u,w}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(X^{\prime}u)^{2q}\right]\right)^{1/(2q)}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(X^{\prime}w)^{2q}\right]\right)^{1/(2q)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\leq\sup_{u,w}\left(\|(X-\mathbb{E}[X])^{\prime}u\|_{2q}+|\mathbb{E}[X^{\prime}u]|\right)\left(\|(X-\mathbb{E}[X])^{\prime}w\|_{2q}+|\mathbb{E}[X^{\prime}w]|\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\lesssim\left(\phi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\right)\left(\phi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\lesssim\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}).

Also, by Assumption
4
,

\displaystyle\|v_{0}(\tau)\|_{2}\leq\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\mathrm{and}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\|\theta_{0}(\tau)-\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\|_{2}\leq L_{\theta}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|.

Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}\leq L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|.

(123)

Bound on
\mathbf{II}
.
By Assumptions
1
and
9
and Lemma
25
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{II}

\displaystyle\leq\bar{f}\|v_{0}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\|_{2}\sup_{u}\left\|X^{\prime}u\right\|_{P,q}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{f}\|v_{0}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\|_{2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{v})

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{v}\bar{f}^{2}L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{v})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}(p)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|,

(124)

where the supremum over is taken over all
u
satisfying
\|u\|_{2}=1
,
\|u\|_{0}\leq 2s_{v}
.

Bound on
\mathbf{III}
.
By By Assumptions
1
and
9
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{III}\leq\bar{f}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sup_{u}\left\|X^{\prime}u\right\|_{P,q}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|\leq\bar{f}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|,

(125)

where the supremum over is taken over all
u
satisfying
\|u\|_{2}=1
,
\|u\|_{0}\leq s_{v}
.

Bound on
\mathbf{IV}
.
We compute

\displaystyle\mathbf{IV}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\left[f_{Y|X}^{q}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})|X)\big|X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\big|^{q}\big|\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}-\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\}\big|\right]\right)^{1/q}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\left[f_{Y|X}^{q}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})|X)\big|X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\big|^{q}\mathbf{1}\{X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})<Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\right]\right)^{1/q}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\left(\mathbb{E}\left[f_{Y|X}^{q}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})|X)\big|X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\big|^{q}\mathbf{1}\{X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)<Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\}\right]\right)^{1/q}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\mathbb{E}\left[f_{Y|X}^{q}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})|X)\big|X^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau)\big|^{q}\big|F_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})|X)-F_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\big|\right]\right)^{1/q}

\displaystyle\leq 2\bar{f}^{1+1/q}\|v_{0}(\tau)\|_{2}\|\theta_{0}(\tau)-\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\|_{2}^{1/q}\sup_{u,w}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[|X^{\prime}u|^{q}|X^{\prime}w|\right]\right)^{1/q},

where the supremum over is taken over all
u,w
satisfying
\|u\|_{2}=\|w\|_{2}=1
,
\|u\|_{0}\leq s_{v}
and
\|w\|_{0}\leq 2s_{\theta}
. By Assumption
1
we can upper bound the expected values in the last displayed line as follows:

\displaystyle\sup_{u,w}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[|X^{\prime}u|^{q}|X^{\prime}w|\right]\right)^{1/q}\leq\sup_{u,w}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[|X^{\prime}u|^{2q}\right]\right)^{1/(2q)}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(X^{\prime}w)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/(2q)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\leq\sup_{u,w}\left(\|(X-\mathbb{E}[X])^{\prime}u\|_{2q}+|\mathbb{E}[X^{\prime}u]|\right)\left(\|(X-\mathbb{E}[X])^{\prime}w\|_{2}+|\mathbb{E}[X^{\prime}w]|\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\lesssim\left(\phi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\right)\left(\phi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\lesssim\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\varphi_{\max}^{1/4}(2s_{\theta}).

Hence, by Assumption
4
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{IV}\leq\bar{f}^{1+1/q}L_{\theta}^{1/q}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\varphi_{\max}^{1/4}(2s_{\theta})\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|^{1/q}.

(126)

Conclusion.
Combining eq. (
I.6
) and the upper bounds (
123
)–(
126
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|(g_{\tau}-Pg_{\tau})-(g_{\tau^{\prime}}-Pg_{\tau^{\prime}})\right\|_{P,q}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\lesssim L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\bar{f}^{2}L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}(p)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\bar{f}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|+\bar{f}^{2}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\varphi_{\max}^{1/4}(2s_{\theta})\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|^{1/q}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\equiv K\rho_{q}(\tau,\tau^{\prime}),

(127)

where
\rho_{q}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})=|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|^{1/q}
(because
\tau,\tau^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}\subseteq(0,1)
). Total boundedness of
\mathcal{G}
in the standard deviation metric now follows from eq. (
I.6
) since
\mathcal{T}
is totally bounded with respect to
\rho_{2}
.

Proof of (ii).
We adapt the approach of the proof of Lemma A.3 in
Chao et al., 2017
to our setting. Let
\bar{q}\in\mathbb{N}
and
q=(q_{1},\ldots,q_{n})^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
. By eq. (
I.6
), we have, for all
g_{\tau},g_{\tau^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{G}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}(g_{\tau}-g_{\tau^{\prime}})\right|^{2\bar{q}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-\bar{q}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\tau}(X_{i},Y_{i})-g_{\tau^{\prime}}(X_{i},Y_{i})\right)^{2\bar{q}}\right]

\displaystyle=n^{-\bar{q}}\sum_{\|q\|_{1}=2\bar{q}}{2\bar{q}\choose q_{1},\ldots,q_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(g_{\tau}(X_{i},Y_{i})-g_{\tau^{\prime}}(X_{i},Y_{i})\big)^{q_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle=n^{-\bar{q}}\sum_{k=1}^{2\bar{q}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|q\|_{1}=2\bar{q},\>q_{i}\geq 2,\\
\|q\|_{0}=k\end{subarray}}{2\bar{q}\choose q_{1},\ldots,q_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(g_{\tau}(X_{i},Y_{i})-g_{\tau^{\prime}}(X_{i},Y_{i})\big)^{q_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-\bar{q}}\sum_{k=1}^{\bar{q}}{n\choose k}(2\bar{q})!K^{2\bar{q}}|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|^{k}

\displaystyle\lesssim(2\bar{q})!K^{2\bar{q}}\sum_{k=1}^{\bar{q}}\left(\frac{e}{k}\right)^{k}\frac{|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|^{k}}{n^{\bar{q}-k}}.

Hence, for all
\tau,\tau^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}
satisfying
|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|\geq 1/n
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{G}_{n}(g_{\tau}-g_{\tau^{\prime}})\right\|_{P,2\bar{q}}

\displaystyle\lesssim K\rho_{2}(\tau,\tau^{\prime}).

(128)

Note that the packing number
D(\epsilon,T,\rho_{2})
the
\epsilon
-packing number of
T
with respect to
\rho_{2}
satisfies

\displaystyle D(\epsilon,T,\rho_{2})\lesssim 1/\epsilon^{2}.

(129)

Thus, by Lemma
35
with
\Psi(x)=x^{12}
,
\eta=\xi^{1/2}
,
\bar{\eta}=n^{-1/2}
(WLOG we can assume that
n
is so large that
n^{-1}<\xi
), and eq. (
128
) and (
129
) there exists a random variable
S_{n}(\xi)
such that

\displaystyle\sup_{|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|\leq\xi}|\mathbb{G}_{n}(g_{\tau}-g_{\tau^{\prime}})|=\sup_{\rho_{2}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\leq\xi^{1/2}}|\mathbb{G}_{n}(g_{\tau}-g_{\tau^{\prime}})|\leq S_{n}(\xi)+2\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\rho_{2}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\leq n^{-1/2}\\
\tau^{\prime}\in\widetilde{T},\tau\in T\end{subarray}}|\mathbb{G}_{n}(g_{\tau}-g_{\tau^{\prime}})|,

(130)

where the set
\widetilde{T}
contains at most
D(n^{-1/2},T,\rho_{2})\lesssim n
points, and

\displaystyle\|S_{n}(\xi)\|_{P,\Psi}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{n^{-1/2}/2}^{\xi^{1/2}}\Psi^{-1}\big(D(\epsilon,T,\rho_{2})\big)d\epsilon+(\xi^{1/2}+2n^{-1/2})\Psi^{-1}\big(D^{2}(\xi,T,\rho_{2})\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{n^{-1/2}/2}^{\xi^{1/2}}\epsilon^{-1/6}d\epsilon+(\xi^{1/2}+2n^{-1/2})\xi^{-1/3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\xi^{5/12}-n^{-5/12}+\xi^{1/6}+n^{-1/2}\xi^{-1/3}.

Hence, for
\varepsilon>0
arbitrary, using Markov’s inequality we bound the first term on the far right hand side of eq. (
130
) by

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{|S_{n}(\xi)|>\varepsilon\right\}\leq\varepsilon^{-12}\left(\xi^{5/12}-n^{-5/12}+\xi^{1/6}+n^{-1/2}\xi^{-1/3}\right)^{12}\rightarrow 0\>\>\mathrm{as}\>\>n\rightarrow\infty\>\>\mathrm{followed\>by}\>\>\xi\rightarrow 0.

(131)

Bounding the second term on the far right hand side of eq. (
130
) is slightly more involved. We have

\displaystyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\rho_{2}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\leq n^{-1/2}\\
\tau^{\prime}\in\widetilde{T},\tau\in T\end{subarray}}|\mathbb{G}_{n}(g_{\tau}-g_{\tau^{\prime}})|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\rho_{2}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\leq n^{-1/2}\\
\tau^{\prime},\tau\in T\end{subarray}}|\mathbb{G}_{n}(g_{\tau}-g_{\tau^{\prime}})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}+\frac{\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}+\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{3}}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{II}+\mathbf{III},

(132)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{1}

\displaystyle=\left\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\big\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\big\}-\tau^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}\big\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\}\right)X^{\prime}v,\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\left.|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|\leq 1/n,\>v\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau,\tau^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=\big(f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)-f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})|X)\big)\left(\tau^{\prime}-\mathbf{1}\big\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\big\}\right)X^{\prime}v,\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\left.|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|\leq 1/n,\>v\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau,\tau^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{3}

\displaystyle=\left\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X,Y)=f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})|X)\left(\tau^{\prime}-\mathbf{1}\big\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\big\}\right)X^{\prime}\big(v_{0}(\tau;z)-v_{0}(\tau^{\prime};z)\big),\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\left.|\tau-\tau^{\prime}|\leq 1/n,\>\tau,\tau^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}\right\}.

Bound on
\mathbf{I}
.
By Lemma
31
with
r_{0}=L_{f}/n
and
s=s_{v}
we have, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)^{2}\frac{\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\Big(\upsilon_{L_{f}/n,n}\big(s_{\theta}\log(n/L_{f})\big)+\upsilon_{L_{f}/n,n}(t_{s_{v},s_{\theta},s_{\theta},n,\delta})\Big)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\lesssim\bar{f}^{3/2}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)^{2}\frac{\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\left(\frac{s_{\theta}\log(n/L_{f})\log(n)}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})\log(n)}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\log(L_{f}L\theta n/\delta)\log(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\right),\end{split}

(133)

where
t_{s,k,s_{\theta},n,\delta}=s\log(ep/s)+k\log(ep/k)+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(L_{f}L_{\theta}n/\delta)
and
\upsilon_{r_{0},n}(z)=\sqrt{z}\big(\sqrt{r_{0}}+n^{-1/2}(\log n)\sqrt{z}+n^{-1}(\log n)^{3/2}z\big)
for
z\geq 0
and the second, simplified inequality follows from the premises of the lemma.

Bound on
\mathbf{II}
.
Let
\{g_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
be a sequence of i.i.d. standard normal random variables independent of
\{(X_{i},Y_{i})\}_{i=1}^{n}
. Define
K(\tau^{\prime\prime})=C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau^{\prime\prime}),2\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1)
and

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}=\left\{f:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\times\mathbb{R}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:f(X,Y,g)=g\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\big\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\}\right)(X^{\prime}v)(X^{\prime}u),\right.

\displaystyle\left.v\in K(\tau),\>\|u\|_{0}\leq 2s_{\theta},\>\|u\|_{2}=1,\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\right\}.

By Lemma
27
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{F}}&\lesssim\bar{c}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{1})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{2})\sqrt{s_{1}\log(ep/s_{1})+s_{2}\log(ep/s_{2})+\log(1/\delta)}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{1+\pi_{n,1/3}^{2}(s_{1}\log(ep/s_{1})+s_{2}\log(ep/s_{2})+\log(1/\delta))},\end{split}

(134)

where
\pi_{n,1/3}^{2}(z)=\sqrt{z/n}+z^{3}/n
with
z\geq 0
. We now turn the bound on this process into a bound on
\mathbf{II}
. For any increasing and convex function
F
we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[F\left(\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}\left[F\left(\sqrt{2\pi}\sup_{\tau,\tau^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{\tau^{\prime}:|\tau^{\prime}-\tau|\leq 1/n}\sup_{v\in K(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{i}\big(f_{i}(\tau)-f_{i}(\tau^{\prime})\big)\big(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\}\big)X_{i}^{\prime}v\right|\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}\left[F\left(4\sqrt{2\pi}L_{f}\sup_{\tau,\tau^{\prime\prime}}\sup_{\tau^{\prime}:|\tau^{\prime}-\tau|\leq 1/n}\sup_{v\in K(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{i}\big(X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)-X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime})\big)\big(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\}\big)X_{i}^{\prime}v\right|\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}\left[F\left(4\sqrt{2\pi}L_{f}L_{\theta}/n\sup_{\tau^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{\|u\|_{0}\leq 2s_{\theta},\>\|u\|_{2}=1}\sup_{v\in K(\tau^{\prime\prime})}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{i}\big(\tau^{\prime\prime}-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\}\big)(X_{i}^{\prime}v)(X_{i}^{\prime}u)\right|\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[F\left(4\sqrt{2\pi}L_{f}L_{\theta}/n\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{F}}\right)\right],

(135)

where
(a)
holds by Lemma 6.3 followed by Lemma 4.5 in
Ledoux and Talagrand, 1996
,
(b)
holds by Lemma
39
(note that by Assumption
5
the map
z\mapsto f_{Y|X}(z-X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)-f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)
is Lipschitz-continuous and vanishes at 0), and
(c)
holds by Assumption
4
.
Thus, by Lemma
38
and eq. (
134
) and (
I.6
), with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{II}&\lesssim L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{1})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{2})\frac{\bar{c}^{2}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sqrt{\frac{s_{1}\log(ep/s_{1})+s_{2}\log(ep/s_{2})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times n^{-1/2}\times\sqrt{1+\pi_{n,1/3}^{2}(s_{1}\log(ep/s_{1})+s_{2}\log(ep/s_{2})+\log(1/\delta))},\end{split}

(136)

where
\pi_{n,1/3}^{2}(z)=\sqrt{z/n}+z^{3}/n
with
z\geq 0
.

Bound on
\mathbf{III}
.
Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}=\left\{h:\mathbb{R}^{p+1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:h(X,Y)=f_{X|Y}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)(\tau-\mathbf{1}\{Y\leq X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\})X^{\prime}v,\right.

\displaystyle\left.v\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\right\}.

By Lemma
25
followed by Lemma
29
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{III}

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{v}\bar{f}L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\frac{\varphi_{\max}(p)}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{n}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{v}\bar{c}\bar{f}^{2}L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\frac{\varphi_{\max}(p)}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{n}\psi_{n}\big(t_{s_{v},s_{\theta},n,\delta}\big)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\lesssim C_{v}\bar{c}\bar{f}^{2}L_{f}L_{\theta}\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(s_{v})\big(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta})\big)\frac{\varphi_{\max}(p)}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta}/\delta}{n}},\end{split}

(137)

where
t_{s_{v},s_{\theta},n,\delta}=s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta}/\delta)
and
\psi_{n}(z)=\sqrt{z}\big(1+n^{-1/2}\sqrt{z}+n^{-3/2}z^{3/2}\big)
for
z\geq 0
, and the last, simplified inequality follows from the premises of the lemma.

Combine eq. (
I.6
)–(
I.6
) and set
\delta=1/n
. Then, for
\varepsilon>0
arbitrary and under the premises of the lemma, the second term in eq. (
130
) satisfies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\rho_{2}(\tau,\tau^{\prime})\leq n^{-1/2}\\
\tau^{\prime}\in\widetilde{T},\tau\in T\end{subarray}}|\mathbb{G}_{n}(g_{\tau}-g_{\tau^{\prime}})|>\varepsilon\right\}\rightarrow 0\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\mathrm{as}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}n\rightarrow\infty,

(138)

Hence, eq. (
131
) and eq. (
138
) imply that, for
\varepsilon>0
arbitrary,

\displaystyle\lim_{\xi\downarrow 0}\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{P}\left\{\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{\xi}}>\varepsilon\right\}=0.

This completes the proof.
∎

Proof of Theorem
7
.

By Theorem 1.5.7 and Addendum 1.5.8 in
van der Vaart and Wellner, 1996
it suffices to establish asymptotic equicontinuity of the leading term of the Bahadur-type representation from Theorem
6
, total boundedness of
\mathcal{G}
with respect to the standard deviation metric, and finite dimensional convergence to a Gaussian random vector. Asymptotic equicontinuity and total boundedness are established in Lemma
20
; hence we are left to show the finite dimensional convergence.

Finite dimensional convergence to a Gaussian random vector follows the strong moment conditions (Assumption
1
), the assumption that the limit covariance function exists, and the Lindeberg Feller CLT combined with the Cramér-Wold theorem. We omit this standard argument; for details see proof of Theorem 2.1 on p. 3292 in
Chao et al., 2017
. We are left to show that the asymptotic mean equals zero and that the (finite dimensional) covariance is as stated in the theorem. First, for all
\tau\in\mathcal{T}
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{Y|X}(X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X_{i})(\tau-1\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)\})X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau;z)\right]\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{Y|X}(X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X_{i})\big(\tau-F_{Y|X}(X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X_{i})\big)X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau;z)\right]\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}=0.\end{split}

(139)

Second, for arbitrary
\tau_{1},\ldots,\tau_{K}\in\mathcal{T}
, the asymptotic covariance matrix is

\displaystyle\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{Y|X}(X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau_{j})|X_{i})(\tau_{j}-1\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau_{j})\})\big(X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau_{j};z)\big)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{Y|X}(X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau_{k})|X_{i})^{2}(\tau_{k}-1\{Y_{i}\leq X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau_{k})\})\big(X_{i}^{\prime}v_{0}(\tau_{k};z)\big)\right]\right)_{j,k=1}^{K}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}=\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\Big((\tau_{j}\wedge\tau_{k}-\tau_{j}\tau_{k})/4v_{0}^{\prime}(\tau_{j};z)\mathbb{E}[f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau_{j})|X)f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau_{k})|X)XX^{\prime}]v_{0}^{\prime}(\tau_{k};z)\Big)_{j,k=1}^{K}

where we have used the definition of
v_{0}(\tau;z)
, Assumption
2
, and eq. (
139
).
∎

Proof of Lemma
21
.

We only prove the special case
\tau_{1}=\tau_{2}=\tau
. The general case follows by the obvious generalization of the arguments; see the comment at the end of this proof.

For notational convenience, we write
v_{0}(\tau)
for
v_{0}(\tau;z)
,
\hat{v}(\tau)
for
\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau;z)
,
f(\tau)
for
f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)
, and
f_{i}(\tau)
for
f_{Y|X}(X_{i}^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X_{i})
.
Define

\displaystyle D(\tau):=\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{E}\left[f^{2}(\tau)XX^{\prime}\right],\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\widetilde{D}(\tau):=\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}^{2}(\tau)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime},\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\mathrm{and}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}\widehat{D}(\tau):=\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{f}_{i}^{2}(\tau)X_{i}X_{i}^{\prime},

and write

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{H}(\tau,\tau;z)-H(\tau,\tau;z)\right|=\left|\hat{v}^{\prime}(\tau)\widehat{D}(\tau)\hat{v}(\tau)-v_{0}^{\prime}(\tau)D(\tau)v_{0}(\tau)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\big(\hat{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{D}(\tau)-D(\tau)\right)\big(\hat{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)\right|+\left|\big(\hat{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}\left(\widehat{D}(\tau)-\widetilde{D}(\tau)\right)\big(\hat{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+2\left|v_{0}^{\prime}(\tau)\left(\widetilde{D}(\tau)-D(\tau)\right)\big(\hat{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)\right|+2\left|v_{0}^{\prime}(\tau)\left(\widehat{D}(\tau)-\widetilde{D}(\tau)\right)\big(\hat{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\left|v_{0}^{\prime}(\tau)\left(\widetilde{D}(\tau)-D(\tau)\right)v_{0}(\tau)\right|+\left|v_{0}^{\prime}(\tau)\left(\widehat{D}(\tau)-\widetilde{D}(\tau)\right)v_{0}(\tau)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\left|\big(\hat{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)^{\prime}D(\tau)\big(\hat{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+2\left|v_{0}^{\prime}(\tau)D(\tau)\big(\hat{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big)\right|

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{II}+\mathbf{III}+\mathbf{IV}+\mathbf{V}+\mathbf{VI}+\mathbf{VII}+\mathbf{VIII}.

(140)

Recall that by Lemmas
23
(i) and
24
,

\displaystyle v_{0}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)

\displaystyle\in\left\{u\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:(u_{T_{v}(\tau)},u_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)})=(0,w_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)}),\>w\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\right\},

(141)

\displaystyle\hat{v}_{\gamma}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)

\displaystyle\in C_{1}^{p}(T_{v}(\tau),2\bar{c})\cup B^{p}_{1}\left(0,\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right),

(142)

and, by Definition
10
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\|v_{0}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)\|_{2}\lesssim\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}r_{a},

(143)

\displaystyle\tilde{v}(\tau)\in C^{p}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\cap B^{p}_{2}\left(0,\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right).

(144)

We now bound the eight terms in eq. (
I.6
).

Bound on
\mathbf{I}
.
Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{1}

\displaystyle=\Big\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)(X^{\prime}u)^{2},\>u\in C^{P}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau,2\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\Big\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)(X^{\prime}u)^{2},\>u\in U^{p}\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\Big\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{3}

\displaystyle=\Big\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)(X^{\prime}u)^{2},\>u\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\>\|u\|_{0}\leq 1,\>\|u\|_{2}\leq 1,\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\Big\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{4}

\displaystyle=\Big\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)(X^{\prime}u)(X^{\prime}w),\>u\in C^{P}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau,2\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}w\in U^{p}\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\Big\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{5}

\displaystyle=\Big\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)(X^{\prime}u)(X^{\prime}w),\>u\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\>\|u\|_{0}\leq 1,\>\|u\|_{2}\leq 1,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}w\in U^{p}\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\Big\},

where
U^{p}:=\left\{u\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:(u_{T_{v}(\tau)},u_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)})=(0,w_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)}),\>w\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\right\}
.

By the geometric constraints (
141
)–(
143
), Lemmas
23
(ii) and (
11
), and Theorem
5
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r_{v}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}+\frac{r_{a}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{3}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\frac{r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{4}}+\frac{r_{v}r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{5}}.

Whence, by Lemma
28
(i) and (iii), and Assumptions
6
and
11
and
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{I}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\left(r_{v}^{2}+\frac{r_{a}^{2}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\frac{r_{a}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+\frac{r_{v}r_{a}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}.\end{split}

(145)

Bound on
\mathbf{II}
.
Recall the definition of function classes
\mathcal{G}_{1},\mathcal{G}_{2},\mathcal{G}_{3},\mathcal{G}_{4}
and define

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{6}

\displaystyle=\Big\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)|X^{\prime}u||X^{\prime}w|,\>u\in C^{P}_{1}(T_{v}(\tau,2\bar{c})\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}w\in U^{p}\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\Big\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{7}

\displaystyle=\Big\{g:\mathbb{R}^{p}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}:g(X)=f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)|X^{\prime}u||X^{\prime}w|,\>u\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\>\|u\|_{0}\leq 1,\>\|u\|_{2}\leq 1,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}w\in U^{p}\cap B^{p}_{2}(0,1),\>\tau\in\mathcal{T}\Big\},

where
U^{p}:=\left\{u\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:(u_{T_{v}(\tau)},u_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)})=(0,w_{T_{v}^{c}(\tau)}),\>w\in C^{p}_{2}(T_{v}(\tau),1)\right\}
.

By the geometric constraints (
141
)–(
143
), Lemmas
23
(ii) and (
11
), Theorem
5
, and Lemma
19
, we have, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{II}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}r_{v}^{2}\|\mathbb{P}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}+r_{f}r_{a}^{2}\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{P}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}+r_{f}\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)^{2}\|\mathbb{P}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{3}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+r_{f}r_{a}\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{P}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{6}}+r_{f}r_{v}r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{P}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{7}}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}\left(\frac{r_{v}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}+\frac{r_{a}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{3}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\frac{r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{6}}+\frac{r_{v}r_{a}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{7}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+r_{f}\left(r_{v}^{2}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\mathbb{E}[g(X)]+r_{a}^{2}\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\mathbb{E}[g(X)]+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)^{2}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{3}}\mathbb{E}[g(X)]\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+r_{a}\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{6}}\mathbb{E}[g(X)]+r_{v}r_{a}\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{7}}\mathbb{E}[g(X)]\right)

Whence, by Lemma
28
(i), (ii), and (iii), and Assumptions
6
and
11
,
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
, and
r_{f}=o(1)
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{II}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times r_{f}\left(r_{v}^{2}+\frac{r_{a}^{2}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\frac{r_{a}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+\frac{r_{v}r_{a}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right).\end{split}

(146)

Bounds on
\mathbf{III}
and
\mathbf{IV}
.
These two terms can be upper bounded by the sum of the upper bounds on terms
\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{V}
and
\mathbf{II}+\mathbf{VI}
.

Bound on
\mathbf{V}
.
Recall the definition of function classes
\mathcal{G}_{1},\mathcal{G}_{2},\mathcal{G}_{4}
. By the geometric constraints (
141
), (
143
) and (
144
), Lemmas
23
(ii) and (
11
),

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)^{2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}+r_{a}^{2}\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}+r_{a}^{2}\frac{4\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{4}}.

Hence, by Lemma
28
, and Assumptions
6
and
11
and
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{V}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\left(\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}+\frac{r_{a}^{2}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\right)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{v}\log(ep/s_{v})+s_{\theta}\log(ep/s_{\theta})+\log(nL_{f}L_{\theta})+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}.\end{split}

(147)

Bound on
\mathbf{VI}
.
Recall the definition of function classes
\mathcal{G}_{1},\mathcal{G}_{2},\mathcal{G}_{6}
. By the geometric constraints (
141
), (
143
) and (
144
), Lemmas
23
(ii) and (
11
), Theorem
5
, and Lemma
19
, we have, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{VI}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}\left(\frac{2\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)^{2}\|\mathbb{P}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}+r_{f}r_{a}^{2}\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{P}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}+r_{f}r_{a}^{2}\frac{4\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{P}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{f}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\frac{2\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)^{2}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}+\frac{r_{a}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{2}}+\frac{r_{a}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{4\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\|\mathbb{G}_{n}\|_{\mathcal{G}_{6}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 9.24994pt{}\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\left(\frac{2\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right)^{2}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\mathbb{E}[g(X)]+r_{a}^{2}\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\mathbb{E}[g(X)]+r_{a}^{2}\frac{4\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{6}}\mathbb{E}[g(X)]\right)

Whence, by Lemma
28
(i), (ii), and (iii), and Assumptions
6
and
11
,
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
, and
r_{f}=o(1)
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{VI}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})^{2}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\times r_{f}\left(\frac{\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}+\frac{r_{a}^{2}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\right).\end{split}

(148)

Bound on
\mathbf{VII}
.
By the geometric constraints (
141
)–(
143
) and Lemmas
23
(ii) and (
11
),

\displaystyle\mathbf{VII}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{\theta})\big\|\hat{v}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{4}\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(1)\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{4}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{\theta})\big\|\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{2}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{\theta})\big\|\hat{v}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big\|_{2}\big\|\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{2}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi^{1/2}_{\max}(s_{\theta})\varphi^{1/2}_{\max}(1)\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\big\|\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big\|_{2}.

Thus, by Theorem
5
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{VII}

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{\theta})\times\left(r_{v}^{2}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)^{2}+\frac{r_{a}^{2}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}+\frac{r_{v}r_{a}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\frac{r_{a}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right).

(149)

Bound on
\mathbf{VIII}
.
By the geometric constraints (
141
)–(
144
) and Lemmas

(ii) and (
11
),

\displaystyle\mathbf{VIII}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{\theta})\big\|\hat{v}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big\|_{2}\big\|\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{2}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi^{1/2}_{\max}(s_{\theta})\varphi^{1/2}_{\max}(1)\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\big\|\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{4}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{\theta})\big\|\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{4}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{\theta})\big\|\tilde{v}(\tau)\big\|_{2}\big\|\tilde{v}(\tau)-v_{0}(\tau)\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{4}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{\theta})\big\|\hat{v}(\tau)-\tilde{v}(\tau)\big\|_{2}\big\|\tilde{v}(\tau)\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{}+\frac{1}{4}(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi^{1/2}_{\max}(s_{\theta})\varphi^{1/2}_{\max}(1)\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\big\|\tilde{v}(\tau)\big\|_{2}.

Hence, by Theorem
5
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{VIII}&\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{\theta})\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt{}\times\left(\frac{r_{a}^{2}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\frac{r_{a}\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}+\frac{r_{a}^{2}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}+\frac{r_{a}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}+\frac{r_{v}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}+\left(\frac{2\bar{c}\|z\|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}(\infty)}\frac{\mu}{\gamma}\right)\frac{\|z\|_{2}}{\kappa_{2}(\infty)}\right).\end{split}

(150)

Conclusion.
Combine eq. (
145
)–(
150
),
s_{\theta}^{2}\log^{2}(np/\delta)\log^{2}(n)=o(n)
, and
r_{f}\vee r_{a}=o(1)
to conclude that, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\widehat{H}(\tau,\tau;z)-H(\tau,\tau;z)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+2\bar{c})\bar{f}^{2}\varphi_{\max}(s_{v})(1+\varphi_{\max}^{1/2}(2s_{\theta}))\times r_{v}.

Under the assumptions of Corollary
3
this upper bound can be further simplified as in the statement of the lemma. This completes the proof.

To establish the claim for
\tau_{1}\neq\tau_{2}
, we replace
f_{Y|X}^{2}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau)|X)
by
f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau_{1})|X)f_{Y|X}(X^{\prime}\theta_{0}(\tau_{2})|X)
in the definitions of the function classes
\mathcal{G}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{G}_{7}
. We then need a generalization of Lemma
28
which handles these function classes. By inspecting the proof of Lemma
28
it is obvious that such a generalization holds true. We omit a formal, lengthy, and uneventful proof.
∎

Proofs of Section

Proof of Lemma
22
.

Only the first statement needs a proof. We compute

\displaystyle\|\tilde{v}_{T^{c}}\|_{2}=\|v_{T^{c}}\|_{2}\leq\alpha\|v_{T}\|_{2}\leq\alpha\frac{|t|}{\beta}\|v_{T}\|_{2}=\frac{\alpha}{\beta}\|\tilde{v}_{T^{c}}\|_{2}.
